## Storage transport failure reproduced — 16 October 2026

At 4872b29 a synthetic HTTPX ReadTimeout escapes SupabaseStorageClient.select
instead of the documented StorageError boundary; RetrievalEngine catches only
StorageError, so network failures can crash retrieval. HTTP status failures
already translate correctly. Student capability: unavailable storage yields a
structured error without evidence or an administrative retry. Path: production
HTTP client → runtime selects/RPC → legacy/source-aware engine; master10 / Task6.

Plan: focused client and composed engine RED tests for transport failures,
translate HTTPX RequestError once at the client boundary, preserve successful
request/permission behavior, verify GREEN plus relevant storage/retrieval tests
and one independent review. No automatic retries or broad exception swallowing.

## Decisions

Latest: record only C1 partial coverage supported by existing confirmations.
Choose an immutable administrative receipt over evidence-ID-only storage so
exact scope is durable. Preserve existing snapshot provenance strings under
admin restrictions rather than rewriting historical type_fields. New full-design
approval authorizes a bounded build only, not source authority or activation.

Owner requires ordinary queries without compulsory undergraduate/source declarations, brief answer citations, latest applicable reviewed sources and clear excluded-scope notices. See2026-10-15-natural-query-response-requirements.md. Similarity is relevance, not authority; original/new benchmark wordings unaccepted.

Latest explicit G03-only confirmation and ASG physical23 exclusion above supersede older all-G-pending notes; original G01/G02 remain unconfirmed.

Owner says the similarity claim was told to them and can be disregarded. It is
unverified hearsay, not policy evidence. Retain documented provisions as candidates;
actual source clauses, campus/department associations and exact review determine
later use. Possible eight-college policy delivery is not a source-acquisition or
completeness claim. User uncertainty means this reply is not exact G verification.
